*Ming Zhong,Xiangyu Ren,Jinglei Cheng,Shaohua Li,Zhiding Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出QuTuner，一个面向量子编译器的特征引导优化通序列调优框架。它通过构建大型优化数据集，结合静态电路特征和优化感知通嵌入来表征电路，训练两个离线模型进行通序列检索与排序，并在Qiskit和PyTKET上实现了比最强基线最高84.85%的指标提升，同时调优时间降低73.59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子编译器通调优方法存在两个局限：一是仅搜索了优化通空间的一小部分，二是主要依赖静态特征，无法显式反映电路对编译器优化的响应。因此需要一种更全面的通调优方法。

Method: QuTuner首先构建大规模优化数据集；然后从两个互补视角表征电路：静态电路特征描述电路结构，优化感知通嵌入总结电路对各个优化通的响应；利用这些表示训练两个离线模型，用于对未见过的电路进行通序列检索和排序，随后进行轻量级精调。

Result: 在Qiskit上，QuTuner相比最强基线将评估指标提升最高84.85%，同时调优时间减少73.59%；在PyTKET上，指标提升最高18.68%，调优时间减少64.49%。

Conclusion: QuTuner为量子编译器提供了一种有效的自适应通调优方法，在提升编译质量的同时显著降低了调优开销。

Abstract: Quantum compilers play a key role in transforming quantum circuits into lower-cost implementations with improved execution fidelity. This process is commonly guided by circuit-level metrics, such as gate counts and circuit depth. Although compiler pass tuning has been widely studied in classical compilation, directly transferring these techniques to quantum compilers is challenging, because quantum programs are expressed as circuits and exhibit optimization behaviors that are shaped by quantum-specific structures. Prior quantum compiler tuning approaches have begun to use circuit features to guide pass selection, but they remain limited in two aspects: they search only a small portion of the optimization-pass space, and they mainly rely on static features that do not explicitly reflect how a circuit reacts to compiler optimizations.
  We present QuTuner, a feature-guided quantum compiler pass tuning framework that generalizes across compilers and tuning objectives. QuTuner first builds a large optimization dataset. It then characterizes each circuit from two complementary views: static circuit features that describe circuit structure, and optimization-aware pass embeddings that summarize the circuit's responses to individual optimization passes. Using these representations, QuTuner trains two offline models to retrieve and rank candidate pass sequences for unseen circuits, followed by lightweight refinement. We evaluate QuTuner on Qiskit and PyTKET using two benchmark suites. On Qiskit, QuTuner improves the evaluation-metric reduction by up to 84.85% over the strongest baseline while reducing tuning time by 73.59%. On PyTKET, it improves metric reduction by up to 18.68% with a 64.49% reduction in tuning time. These results show that QuTuner provides an effective approach to adaptive pass tuning for quantum compilers.

</details>


### [102] [Breaking the One-Dimensional Expressibility-Trainability Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04598)
*Kyoungho Cho,Yu-Seong Jeon,Jinhyoung Lee,Jeongho Bang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过建立矩层次（moment hierarchy）分析框架，揭示了参数化量子电路中表达能力与训练性之间的所谓"一维权衡"实际上是一种误解。EP（纠缠能力）衡量全局两拷贝平均，EPD（纠缠能力偏差）衡量全局四拷贝波动，而梯度方差是局部两拷贝压缩量。三者可分离，因此高表达能力不一定导致贫瘠高原（BP），为设计高表达性且可训练的PQC提供了实用准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决参数化量子电路设计中表达能力与训练性之间的表面矛盾：通常认为提高希尔伯特空间覆盖（表达能力）会随机化电路并激活贫瘠高原条件，但这种"一维权衡"可能掩盖了更细致的结构。

Method: 通过矩层次分析区分三种不同量：EP（纠缠能力）作为全局两拷贝平均、EPD（纠缠能力偏差）作为全局四拷贝波动、梯度方差作为局部两拷贝收缩量。使用Haar-积输入固定电路，通过参数光锥和代价可观测量分析梯度方差。

Result: 发现相同EP的电路可以具有不同的EPD和梯度方差，证明表达能力不等同于训练性；电路可在EPD和梯度方差坍塌前达到高Haar-like覆盖率，说明覆盖率和BP激活是不同交叉事件。

Conclusion: EP/EPD框架将一维表达能力-训练性权衡转化为实用的二维设计准则：在覆盖率已高但BP均匀化尚未消除可训练结构的窗口中搜索高表达性PQC。

Abstract: Expressive parameterized quantum circuits (PQCs) are often designed under a dilemma: the growth of expressibility and entangling power (EP) that improves Hilbert-space coverage is also expected to randomize an ansatz and activate barren-plateau (BP) conditions. We show that this dilemma is not a one-dimensional tradeoff. The usual picture collapses three inequivalent objects -- parameter-ensemble coverage, fixed-circuit entangling response, and local gradient moments -- into one scalar narrative. For a fixed circuit probed by Haar-product inputs, EP is a global two-copy mean of the output-entanglement distribution, whereas entangling-power deviation (EPD) is a global four-copy fluctuation descriptor. Gradient variance, however, is a local two-copy contraction selected by a parameter light cone and a cost observable. This moment hierarchy yields an analytic separation: equal EP need not imply equal trainability, as witnessed by equal-EP circuits with different EPDs and different gradient variances. These separations turn EP and EPD into a two-dial design rule for PQC ansatzes: EP measures how far the circuit has moved along the coverage dial, while EPD monitors whether input-dependent variability remains. We find that ansatz routes can reach high, Haar-like coverage before EPD and gradient variance collapse, showing that coverage and BP activation are distinct crossover events. The EP/EPD framework thus breaks the apparent one-dimensional expressibility-trainability tradeoff into a practical design rule: search for highly expressive PQCs in the window where coverage is high but BP-like homogenization has not yet erased trainable structure.

</details>


### [103] [Measurement Geometry as a Resource for Certifying Network Nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04656)
*Leon Adachi,Le Bin Ho*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在双局域Alice-Bob-Charlie量子网络中，利用辅助比特辅助测量建立了认证网络非局域性的操作框架，并在156量子比特超导处理器上实验验证了双局域非局域性和完全网络非局域性，揭示了测量几何作为网络非局域性独立资源的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然量子网络中纠缠的角色已被充分理解，但测量设计对网络非局域性的影响尚待探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立测量几何作为网络非局域性独立资源的理论框架。

Method: 提出使用辅助比特辅助测量（ancilla-assisted meters）来评估双局域和完全网络非局域（FNN）证词所需的非局域可观测量；在156量子比特超导处理器ibm_kingston上进行实验验证。

Result: 观察到双局域非局域性$\mathcal{S}_{\rm BLHV}=1.067(6)>1$（经读取误差缓解）；FNN证词达到认证阈值的99%和96%；证明Bob的联合测量决定可实现的网络非局域性水平，不同测量设置分别优化双局域和FNN认证。

Conclusion: 测量几何是网络非局域性的独立资源，为在可编程量子处理器上认证网络非局域性提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Quantum networks can exhibit nonclassical correlations that cannot be explained by classical models with independent sources. While the role of entanglement is well understood, the impact of measurement design remains largely unexplored. Here we develop an operational framework for certifying network nonlocality in the bilocal Alice--Bob--Charlie network using ancilla-assisted meters to evaluate the nonlocal observables required for bilocal and fully network nonlocal (FNN) witnesses. The approach successfully reproduces both bilocal and FNN correlations in simulation. On the 156-qubit superconducting processor \textit{ibm\_kingston}, we observe bilocal nonlocality with $\mathcal{S}_{\rm BLHV}=1.067(6)>1$ after readout-error mitigation, while the FNN witnesses reach $99\%$ and $96\%$ of their certification thresholds, implying the substantially stronger requirements for FNN certification. We further show that Bob's joint measurement determines the accessible level of network nonlocality: bilocal and FNN certification are optimized by different measurement settings, while both violations can disappear even for maximally entangled states. These results identify measurement geometry as an independent resource for network nonlocality and provide a practical route toward its certification on programmable quantum processors.

</details>


### [104] [Krylov complexity, mode-resolved complexity and entanglement entropy across phase transitions in the non-Hermitian extended Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04659)
*Ling-Feng Zhang,Wing Chi Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究扩展SSH模型中次近邻跳跃和虚交错化学势引起的非厄米相变，利用Krylov展开复杂度和纠缠熵识别相变，并引入动量分辨复杂度揭示相变细节结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非厄米扩展SSH模型中的相变特征，寻找能够有效标识相变的动力学可观测量，并揭示纯虚能谱区域的动力学相结构。

Method: 采用Krylov展开复杂度和纠缠熵分析两种动力学协议：(i)通过幺正变换制备非厄米基态；(ii)在非厄米哈密顿量下演化系统。引入动量分辨复杂度追踪特征模式随驱动参数的演化。

Result: 在第一种协议中，展开复杂度可有效标识相变；在第二种协议中，长时间展开复杂度和纠缠熵可标识相变。动量分辨复杂度能识别特征模式并追踪其演化。在纯虚能谱区域，基于展开复杂度的饱和行为识别出动力学相，纠缠熵也表现出类似饱和行为。

Conclusion: Krylov展开复杂度和纠缠熵是非厄米拓扑相变的有效探针，动量分辨复杂度提供了更精细的结构信息，纠缠熵可作为动力学相更易实验测量的手段。

Abstract: We investigate phase transitions in the extended Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model with next-nearest-neighbor hoppings and an imaginary staggered chemical potential. In the presence of small non-Hermiticity, exceptional points emerge in pairs from the gap-closing momenta near the topological phase boundaries of the Hermitian limit. Utilizing the Krylov spread complexity and entanglement entropy, we analyze two dynamical protocols: (i) preparing the non-Hermitian ground state via a unitary transformation, and (ii) evolving the system under the non-Hermitian Hamiltonian. We show that the spread complexity, and long-time spread complexity as well as entanglement entropy can effectively signal phase transitions in the first and second protocols, respectively. To unravel the detailed structure of the transitions, we introduce the momentum-resolved complexity that identifies the characteristic modes and tracks their evolution with the driving parameter. In the regime where the system possesses a purely imaginary spectrum, we further identify dynamical phases based on the saturation behavior of the spread complexity. The entanglement entropy is also found to exhibit similar saturation behavior, thereby providing a more experimentally accessible probe of the dynamical phases.

</details>


### [105] [Estimation of a sparse multi-qubit Hamiltonian via compressed sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04669)
*Juntao Tu,Yuanlong Wang,Shuming Cheng,Shuixin Xiao,Zhibo Hou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于压缩感知和“尺度变换”技术的多量子比特哈密顿量估计方法，能够利用仅O(k log(4^N/k))个方程估计k稀疏哈密顿量，解决了N≥3时RIP条件失效的难题，并在6和30量子比特系统中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N量子比特哈密顿量包含4^N-1个未知参数，需要指数级数量的方程才能提取信息，传统压缩感知的RIP充分条件在N≥3时常常失效，阻碍了压缩感知在多量子比特系统中的应用。

Method: 基于压缩感知框架，提出“尺度变换”技术来恢复RIP条件，从而实现对k稀疏哈密顿量的压缩估计，所需方程数量仅为O(k log(4^N/k))。

Result: 在6量子比特和30量子比特两个系统的数值实验中，该方法成功估计了哈密顿量，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的尺度变换技术有效解决了高维量子系统中RIP失效的问题，使得压缩感知能够用于多量子比特哈密顿量的高效估计。

Abstract: Hamiltonian estimation is an effective approach in studying the structure and dynamical evolution of quantum systems. The difficulty in estimating the Hamiltonian is that an $N$-qubit Hamiltonian has $4^N-1$ unknown parameters, requiring exponentially many equations for information extraction. In this paper we develop a method based on compressed sensing to estimate the Hamiltonian of a multi-qubit system. We identify a problem where as $N$ increases, the common sufficient condition (Restricted Isometry Property) for compressed sensing often fails, obstructing the application of compressed sensing in ($N\geq 3$)-qubit Hamiltonian estimation. To solve this problem, we propose a ``scale transformation" technique to restore RIP and ensure a compressive estimation of a $k$-sparse Hamiltonian using only $O(k\log(4^N/k))$ equations. In the numerical examples, we estimate the Hamiltonians of two 6- and 30-qubit systems, demonstrating the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [106] [A Path-Superposition Framework for Quantum Gate Teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04672)
*Santiago Ávila,Marco Enríquez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径叠加的确定性量子门隐形传态通用框架，通过预共享最大纠缠资源和路径依赖的局部幺正算符实现非局域量子操作，并给出了CNOT和CZ门的协议示例及光子学实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现分布式量子计算中非局域量子操作的高效执行，避免物理传输参与量子比特所带来的开销与限制。

Method: 基于路径叠加（path superposition）构建通用框架，利用预共享最大纠缠资源的相位和路径依赖的局部幺正算符族来指定目标非局域操作；通过测量控制量子比特并施加局部修正操作实现确定性隐形传态。

Result: 建立了保证确定性隐形传态的一般设计条件；构造了CNOT门和CZ门的隐形传态协议，证明不同非局域操作可在同一协议架构下通过设计参数选择实现；给出了基于空间路径和偏振自由度的光子学概念验证方案。

Conclusion: 路径叠加是一种实现量子门隐形传态和分布式量子信息处理的通用资源，所提框架为构建确定性非局域量子操作提供了系统化设计方法。

Abstract: Quantum gate teleportation enables distant parties to implement nonlocal quantum operations without physically transferring the participating qubits, making it a promising primitive for distributed quantum computing. We introduce a general framework for deterministic quantum gate teleportation based on path superposition, in which the target nonlocal operation is specified through the phase of a preshared maximally entangled resource and a suitable family of path-dependent local unitary operators. The framework establishes general design conditions that guarantee deterministic teleportation after measurement of the control qubits and the application of local correction operations. As representative realizations, we construct teleportation protocols for controlled-NOT (CNOT) and controlled-Z (CZ) gates, demonstrating that different nonlocal operations can be implemented within the same protocol architecture through appropriate choices of the design parameters. We further outline a proof-of-concept photonic realization based on spatial-path and polarization degrees of freedom. The proposed framework identifies path superposition as a versatile resource for quantum gate teleportation and distributed quantum information processing.

</details>


### [107] [Mapping open quantum dynamics onto graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04721)
*Kyuho Kim,Dayeong Lee,Seungkyun Park,Xianji Piao,Namkyoo Park,Sunkyu Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用的图论框架用于马尔可夫开放量子动力学，将开放量子系统映射到两个唯一定义的图上，实现了从图拓扑角度分析耗散、耦合跃迁等量子现象，并展示了图剪枝在增强图神经网络学习方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放量子动力学方法主要依赖代数形式（部分跳变算符或统计性普适热库），忽略了系统-浴耦合背后的连通性图结构，缺乏一个统一的图论视角来理解开放量子动力学的高维复杂性。

Method: 提出了一个通用的图论框架，将马尔可夫开放量子动力学映射到两个唯一定义的图上，将量子主方程解释为算子值信号在图上的平均波动特性。应用于开放量子Rabi模型，推广了Fock态格点，刻画了耗散的图拓扑特征，并分类了弱到超强耦合转变。进一步通过图剪枝提取动力学骨架用于图神经网络学习。

Result: 在开放量子Rabi模型中实现了Fock态格点的开放系统推广；表征了耗散的图拓扑特征；完成了弱到超强耦合转变的分类；图剪枝揭示了开放量子动力学的骨干结构，提升了图神经网络的学习性能。

Conclusion: 该框架桥接了图论与开放量子动力学，实现了对高维复杂性的高效数据驱动分析，为开放量子系统的拓扑理解和机器学习分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph-theoretic frameworks have been widely employed in quantum physics to address the high-dimensional complexity of quantum systems. Although open quantum dynamics incorporates system-bath coupling via numerous interacting operators, it has been formulated algebraically with a partial set of jump operators or statistically universal reservoirs, leaving the underlying connectivity structure largely unexplored. Here, we propose a universal graph-theoretic framework for Markovian quantum dynamics. The framework maps open quantum dynamics onto two uniquely defined graphs, where the quantum master equation is rigorously interpreted as the average wave characteristic of operator-valued signals across the graphs. Applying this framework to the open quantum Rabi model, we demonstrate an open-system generalization of Fock-state lattices, characterize graph-topological signatures of dissipation, and classify the weak-to-ultrastrong coupling transition. Building on these representations, graph pruning reveals the backbone of open quantum dynamics, which enables superior graph neural-network learning. Our results bridge graph theory and open quantum dynamics, achieving efficient data-driven analysis of high-dimensional complexity.

</details>


### [108] [Magnetic graphs for cavity quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04736)
*Sunkyu Yu,Xianji Piao,Namkyoo Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种磁性图模型，将单原子腔量子电动力学中的量子拉比模型映射到Floquet边界条件下的复杂二分图上，通过子图连接性成本这一单一指标刻画从弱耦合到深强耦合的转变，揭示了子图拓扑诱导的相位阻塞是驱动这一连接性跃迁的核心机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在腔量子电动力学中，增强光-物质耦合是实现超快量子门操作、量子比特保护和确定性非线性光学的核心挑战。随着耦合强度进入超强乃至深强耦合区域，即使是最简单的两能级原子与量子化场相互作用系统也需要精细处理，传统方法面临困难，亟需新的理论框架来统一描述不同耦合区间的量子动力学。

Method: 提出磁性图模型，将广义量子拉比模型映射为Floquet边界条件下的复杂二分图；定义非磁性子图断开成本作为单一度量指标；通过子图拓扑分析揭示相位阻塞机制。

Result: 成功建立了图论与腔QED之间的桥梁，发现从弱耦合到深强耦合的转变可由图论中的子图连接性成本统一刻画，并识别出子图拓扑诱导的相位阻塞是驱动这一连接性跃迁的根本原因。

Conclusion: 该图论框架不仅适用于单原子系统，且可扩展到多体系统，揭示了即使是最简单的量子光-物质相互作用系统也蕴含高度复杂的图动力学行为，为超强耦合腔QED提供了全新的理论视角和分析工具。

Abstract: Strengthening light-matter coupling has become a central challenge in cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED), enabling ultrafast gate operations, qubit protection, and deterministic nonlinear optics. As the coupling increases, even the simplest configuration, a two-level atom interacting with a quantized field, requires careful treatment, as exemplified by the gauge-invariant quantum Rabi model (QRM). Here we propose a magnetic graph model for single-atom cavity QED, which enables the interpretation of quantum dynamics across the ultrastrong coupling regime through graph connectivity. We demonstrate that the generalized QRM maps onto a complex bipartite graph of identical sites under Floquet boundary conditions. This framework captures the crossover from weak to deep-strong coupling via a single metric: the cost of disconnecting a nonmagnetic subgraph. We examine the mechanism underlying this connectivity transition, establishing phase frustration induced by subgraph topology as the primary driver. Scalable to many-body systems, this approach bridges graph theory and cavity QED, revealing highly complex-graph dynamics even in the simplest setting.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum-Optical Bound States in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04742)
*Ruo Kun Cai,Zhi Jiao Deng,Chun Wang Wu,Ping Xing Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个量子光学模型，将多能级Jaynes-Cummings系统映射为Fock态格点，通过两个半无限非均匀SSH链的拓扑零模式叠加形成连续谱束缚态（BIC），并给出利用单个 trapped ion 的实验方案与光谱学探测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIC在经典波系统中已被广泛实现，但在量子光学领域中尚不清楚其精确类比物及如何提取实验信号。

Method: 构建一个驱动的多能级Jaynes-Cummings系统，利用Fock态格点映射将其等效为两个半无限非均匀SSH链耦合到共同连续谱的结构；通过两个拓扑零模式的量子叠加形成BIC；利用手征对称性算符的含时动力学傅里叶变换提取BIC的光谱特征。

Result: 该模型成功在量子光学系统中实现了BIC，该BIC通过破坏性量子干涉与连续谱完全退耦，并在连续谱中保持完美局域化；给出了光谱学探测方法及单个 trapped ion 实验方案。

Conclusion: 该工作将BIC物理与量子光学领域建立桥梁，为在量子极限下利用这类奇特态开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) are counterintuitive localized states that lie within the continuum of extended states. While extensively realized and utilized in classical wave systems, it is still unclear what a close analog of BICs would be, and how to extract their experimental signature in quantum-optical settings -- where the wave field itself is quantized into bosonic excitations. Here, we present a paradigmatic quantum-optical model consisting of a driven multi-level Jaynes-Cummings (JC) system, featuring few quantum degrees of freedom yet capable of hosting a BIC. Using the concept of a Fock-state lattice (FSL), this model can be mapped to an extended structure comprising two semi-infinite inhomogeneous Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chains coupled to a common continuum. An appropriate quantum superposition of two topological zero modes from the separate chains forms a BIC that remains perfectly localized in the Fock-state dimension within the continuum spectrum, due to complete decoupling from the common continuum via destructive quantum interference. We further develop a method to extract the spectroscopic signature of the BIC -- a discrete peak embedded in a continuous background -- by Fourier-transforming the time-dependent dynamics of the system's chiral-symmetry operator. A highly feasible experimental proposal using a single trapped ion is provided. Our work bridges BIC physics with quantum optics, opening a pathway to harnessing such exotic states at the quantum limit.

</details>


### [110] [Sector-memory obstruction to probe-level bath emergence in finite programmable qubit environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04791)
*Gaurav Sarmah,Ramakrishna Podila*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限量子环境中探针与可编程浴池的弛豫行为，发现电荷守恒导致希尔伯特空间分扇区，扇区内平衡不足以产生全局Gibbs态，通过数值模拟和IBM量子硬件实验验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限量子环境不能像正则热库那样使局域探针弛豫，本文旨在通过可控的量子系统研究这一区别，揭示电荷守恒约束下扇区化对探针平衡态的影响。

Method: 采用探针量子比特与N个浴池量子比特耦合的激发数守恒模型，通过扇区解析的晚期布居、扇区记忆方差和全局Gibbs拟合误差三个指标刻画浴池涌现；使用Haar随机纯态进行精确模拟，构造荷守Floquet电路并在IBM Fez量子处理器上实现有限深度实验。

Result: 扇区依赖的布居接近最大混合扇区基准(p_e^(q)=q/(N+1))，产生非零Gibbs阻碍；硬件实验(N=4, ε=0)得到M_N≈0.044, Δ_G^global≈0.558, 电荷保真度约0.90；对称性破缺Rx旋转降低两个诊断指标并增加电荷泄漏，但无法擦除扇区排序。

Conclusion: 约束扇区内的平衡不足以产生单一扇区无关的探针Gibbs态，量子浴池的涌现需要超越扇区化约束的条件。

Abstract: Finite quantum environments can relax local probes without acting as canonical baths. We study this distinction for a probe qubit coupled to a programmable bath of ($N$) qubits under excitation-number-conserving dynamics. The conserved charge partitions the Hilbert space into sectors. We characterize probe-level bath emergence using the sector-resolved late-time population ($p_e^{(q)}$), the sector-memory variance ($M_N$), and a global Gibbs-fit error ($Δ_G^{\mathrm{global}}$). Exact simulations with Haar-random pure states in each complete fixed-charge sector yield sector-dependent populations close to the maximally mixed-sector benchmark ($p_e^{(q)}=q/(N+1)$), producing a nonzero Gibbs obstruction. We then construct charge-preserving Floquet circuits using ($R_z$) phases and ($XX+YY$) exchange gates, validate them with ideal and noisy Qiskit simulations, and implement finite-depth experiments on IBM Fez. For ($N=4$) and ($ε=0$), the hardware data give ($M_N \simeq 0.044$), ($Δ_G^{\mathrm{global}} \simeq 0.558$), and charge preservation near 0.90 after readout mitigation. A paired symmetry-breaking scan using bath ($R_x(ε)$) rotations reduces both diagnostics while increasing charge leakage, but does not erase sector ordering over the accessible depths. These results show that equilibration within constrained sectors is insufficient to produce a single sector-independent Gibbs state for the probe.

</details>


### [111] [HamQASBench: A Hamiltonian-Informed Diagnostic Benchmark for Evaluating Quantum Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04845)
*Jiayang Niu,Akib Karim,Yan Wang,Jie Li,Ke Deng,Azadeh Alavi,Muhammad Usman,Yongli Ren*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出HamQASBench，一个基于哈密顿量信息的量子架构搜索诊断基准，通过分子结构分层和关键结构提取机制，揭示传统能量指标无法检测的五种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QAS基准按分子身份或量子比特数组织实例，这些标准与哈密顿结构无关，且仅依赖能量精度评估，无法检测过参数化等结构性失败。

Method: 基于泡利算符基、计算基表示和基态纠缠导出的分子指纹，将11个分子组织为5个结构层级；设计后验关键结构提取过程，识别满足各层级要求的最小电路；结合每量子比特纠缠分析和成对态保真度补充能量评估。

Result: 基准测试五种QAS方法揭示了五种传统指标不可见的失败模式：极简主义下的过参数化、简并下的本征态锁定、强关联系统中的表示瓶颈、拓扑诱导的路由失败以及电路搜索空间增长的可扩展性瓶颈。

Conclusion: HamQASBench提供了比单纯能量精度更有诊断价值的结构化评估框架，能够暴露QAS方法在电路结构和哈密顿量匹配方面的深层次问题。

Abstract: Quantum Architecture Search (QAS) automates the design of parameterized quantum circuits for variational quantum algorithms, yet existing benchmarks organize instances by molecular identity or qubit count -- criteria agnostic to Hamiltonian structure -- and rely solely on energy accuracy, which cannot detect structural failures such as over-parameterization on near-product ground states. We introduce HamQASBench, a Hamiltonian-informed diagnostic benchmark organizing 11 molecules into five structural tiers via fingerprints derived from the Pauli operator basis, computational basis representation, and ground-state entanglement. A post-hoc critical-structure extraction procedure identifies minimal circuits consistent with each tier's requirements, complementing energy-based evaluation with per-qubit entanglement analysis and pairwise state fidelity. Benchmarking five QAS methods across four paradigms reveals failure modes invisible to conventional metrics: over-parameterization in the minimalism regime, eigenstate commitment under degeneracy, a representation bottleneck in strongly correlated systems, topology-induced routing failure, and circuit search space growth as a scalability bottleneck.

</details>


### [112] [Emergence of the Scrooge Ensemble in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04864)
*Zeyu Liu,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用可解的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型揭示了多体系统中量子测量后投影系综收敛到Scrooge系综的机制，通过路径积分公式解析证明了所有矩与Scrooge系精确匹配，并联系到测量路径积分的鞍点结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多体波函数在部分测量后形成的投影系综的统计结构，特别是"深度热化"现象中Scrooge系综涌现的物理机制。

Method: 利用可解SYK模型，将测量概率和后测量态表述为路径积分形式，解析计算投影系综的所有矩，并与Scrooge系综进行对比；通过分析测量路径积分的鞍点结构揭示副本置换的统计来源。

Result: 解析证明了SYK模型中投影系综的所有矩与Scrooge系综精确匹配，即使在短时间演化下也成立；展示了测量路径积分的鞍点结构自然生成Scrooge统计所需的副本置换。

Conclusion: 建立了可解SYK模型作为研究混沌多体动力学中量子测量普适统计的可处理框架。

Abstract: The probabilistic nature of quantum measurement provides a direct window into the structure and complexity of many-body wave functions. When only part of a system is measured, the remaining degrees of freedom form an ensemble of post-measurement states whose statistical structure can reveal a stronger form of thermalization, known as deep thermalization. Recent numerical evidence suggests that this phenomenon is characterized by convergence of the projected ensemble to the Scrooge ensemble, a maximally random ensemble compatible with a given density matrix. In this Letter, we use the solvable Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model to unveil the mechanism by which the Scrooge ensemble emerges in many-body systems. By formulating measurement probabilities and post-measurement states in terms of path integrals, we analytically characterize all moments of the projected ensemble and show that they exactly match those of the Scrooge ensemble, even at short evolution times. We further connect this result to the saddle-point structure of the measurement path integral, which naturally generates the replica permutations underlying Scrooge statistics. Our results establish the solvable SYK model as a tractable setting for exploring universal statistics of quantum measurements in chaotic many-body dynamics.

</details>


### [113] [Photonic Cluster State Generation from a Quantum Dot Emitting in the Telecom C-band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04896)
*Giora Peniakov,Reza Hekmati,Johannes Michl,Mohamed Helal,Moritz Meinecke,Jochen Kaupp,Yorick Reum,Martin Kamp,Monika Emmerling,Andreas Theo Pfenning,Sven Hoefling,Tobias Huber-Loyola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在电信C波段实现确定性光子纠缠簇态生成，利用砷化铟量子点中的空穴自旋重复激发，过程保真度达0.71，自旋-光子纠缠负性0.27，光子不可区分性≥83%，为与硅光子学兼容的量子通信优势奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前光子簇态的确定性生成局限于近红外波段，而要实现量子通信优势并保持与硅光子学的兼容性，需在电信波长波段运行。

Method: 在外部磁场下，通过重复激发嵌入砷化铟量子点中的空穴自旋，在电信C波段直接生成确定性簇态；通过测量过程映射图进行量子过程表征。

Result: 簇态生成过程保真度F=0.71±0.01；自旋-光子偏振纠缠负性N=0.27±0.02；光子不可区分性至少83%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在电信波段实现确定性簇态生成的可行性，且高光子不可区分性为未来实现超越线性连接的光子簇态融合门提供了潜力。

Abstract: Photonic cluster states are a key resource for photonic quantum information processing. So far, deterministic generation of these states has been limited to the near-infrared wavelength range. To achieve quantum advantage in communication while maintaining compatibility with silicon photonics, operation in the telecom wavelength range is required. In this work, we demonstrate deterministic cluster state generation directly in the telecom C-band. This is achieved through repetitive excitation of a hole spin confined in an indium-arsenide quantum dot subjected to an external magnetic field. We characterize the quantum process that generates the cluster state by measuring its process map, obtaining a fidelity of $\mathrm{F} = 0.71 \pm 0.01$ to the ideal case. As part of this characterization, we observe spin--photon polarization entanglement with a negativity of $\mathrm{N} = 0.27 \pm 0.02$. The emitted photons exhibit indistinguishability of at least 83%, demonstrating the potential for future fusion gates necessary for photonic cluster state generation beyond linear connectivity.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum orientation, Noether structure, composition of systems and operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04899)
*Heinz-Jürgen Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证了除了量子理论的统计结构外，还需要一种称为“诺特结构”的额外结构来描述系统组成和定义完全正操作。诺特结构体现了厄米算子作为可观测量和对称生成元的双重角色。研究表明，在纯操作情况下，完全正操作可被刻画为保持定向的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证量子理论中除了统计结构外，还需要诺特结构来描述系统组成和完全正操作，并建立与Alfsen和Shultz工作的联系。

Method: 在有限维情况下研究诺特结构与系统组成的关系，建立与完全正操作的联系，并将纯操作情况下的完全正操作刻画为保持定向的映射。

Result: 建立了诺特结构与系统组成及完全正操作之间的关联；在纯操作情况下，完全正操作可被刻画为保持定向的映射。

Conclusion: 诺特结构是量子理论中除统计结构外必不可少的组成部分，对于理解系统组成和完全正操作具有重要意义。

Abstract: In this paper we argue that, in addition to the statistical structure of quantum theory, another structure, referred to here as the ``Noether structure," is necessary to describe the composition of systems and to define completely positive operations. A Noether structure reflects the dual role of Hermitian operators as observables on the one hand and as generators of symmetry transformations on the other. This idea has been expressed in a similar form in the works of Alfsen and Shultz, who investigated the conditions under which the Jordan product can be extended to an associative product of operator algebras. Our investigations into the Noether structure and the composition of systems are limited to the finite-dimensional case and establish a connection to completely positive operations. In the case of pure operations, the latter can be characterized as orientation-preserving maps.

</details>


### [115] [Error Mitigation in Bosonic Systems via Virtual Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04914)
*Leonardo Finocchiaro,Marco Robbio,Diogo Gomes,David Gunn,Adithi Udupa,Axel M. Eriksson,Leonardo Novo,Giulia Ferrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了玻色子量子信息处理与连续变量量子计算中虚拟蒸馏（virtual distillation）的理论框架，基于无源线性光学干涉仪实现循环移位算符的对角化，推导了可实验实现的协议，用于估计数算符、相移算符和任意正交分量的虚拟蒸馏期望值，并针对光子损耗和退相干的噪声抑制进行了量化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将原本局限于量子比特系统的虚拟蒸馏误差抑制技术推广到玻色子量子计算架构中，解决该领域缺乏有效误差缓解方案的问题。

Method: 利用无源线性光学干涉仪对角化循环移位算符，基于多副本测量设计协议，通过特征函数估计任意阶数算符关联函数，并针对光子损耗和退相干两种主要噪声机制分析误差抑制效果。

Result: 建立了玻色子系统中虚拟蒸馏的可实验实现协议，能够从多副本测量中恢复数算符、相移算符和任意正交分量的噪声缓解期望值，并量化了噪声贡献的抑制程度。

Conclusion: 将虚拟蒸馏扩展至玻色子量子处理领域，为利用现有线性光学资源实现连续变量量子计算中的误差缓解提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Virtual distillation is a promising error-mitigation technique that exploits multiple copies of a noisy quantum state to estimate observables as if measured on a purified state. Although originally introduced in the context of bosonic many-body systems under the name of virtual cooling, its development and applications have largely focused on qubit-based quantum computation. Here, we establish a framework for virtual distillation in bosonic quantum information processing and continuous-variable quantum computing. Building on a diagonalization of cyclic shift operators implemented with passive linear-optical interferometers, we derive experimentally accessible protocols for estimating virtually distilled expectation values of observables relevant to bosonic architectures. In particular, we show how to recover noise-mitigated expectation values of number operators, phase-shift operators, and arbitrary quadratures from multi-copy measurements. For number operators, we further demonstrate the estimation of virtually distilled correlators of arbitrary order through the characteristic function of the photon-number distribution. We apply the framework to states affected by photon loss and dephasing, two of the dominant noise mechanisms in bosonic quantum computation, and quantify the resulting suppression of noise contributions. Our results extend virtual distillation beyond its original setting and provide a practical route toward error-mitigated measurements in bosonic quantum processors using experimentally available linear-optical resources.

</details>


### [116] [How Hard Is Quantum Advantage? A Cloud Microphysics Stress Test for Variational Quantum Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04915)
*Felix Herbort,Ellen Sarauer,Daniel Ohl de Mello,Paul Christiansen,Steffen Hien,Cedric Brügmann,Dieter Jaksch,Veronika Eyring,Martin Kiffner,Mierk Schwabe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合量子神经网络（QNN）在云微物理数据集上的应用，发现尽管通过超参数优化和频率谱增强可提升性能，但QNN仍被简单全连接神经网络超越，指出了变分量子模型在机器学习中的瓶颈与改进需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习虽有潜力利用量子优势，但缺乏实际改进和可扩展性的强证据，部分由于贫瘠高原等现象。本文旨在探索QNN在复杂物理系统（云微物理）中的可行性，并评估其实际表现。

Method: 采用混合量子神经网络（QNN），结合可训练频率谱和增强表达能力的经典后处理，对包含水相变及温度变化的云微物理数据集进行建模，并进行广泛的超参数优化。

Result: QNN通过超参数优化受益显著，证明了其在复杂物理系统中的可行性，但性能仍低于简单的全连接神经网络基线。

Conclusion: 变分量子模型在学习复杂数据集时存在瓶颈，需要进一步理解和改进，以期在未来填补经典模型失败或低效的领域。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) could have the potential to leverage advantages of quantum over classical computing but still lacks strong evidence of actual improvements and scalability, partly due to phenomena such as barren plateaus. In this paper, we employ a hybrid quantum neural network (QNN) on a dataset on cloud microphysics, containing processes for phase transitions of water in the atmosphere and its related temperature changes, which are highly relevant for accurate climate predictions and projections. To reach optimal performance of our QNNs, we employ a rich and trainable frequency spectrum together with expressivity enhancing classical postprocessing. We find that our QNNs strongly benefit from extensive hyperparameter optimization and thereby demonstrate the feasibility of applying QNNs to complex physical systems. At the same time, the QNNs are outperformed by classical baselines in the form of simple fully-connected neural networks. We discuss identified bottlenecks of this class of quantum models to learn the full complexity of the cloud microphysics dataset to show that there is a need to further understand and improve variational quantum models for machine learning such that they might fill the gap where classical models fail or are inefficient.

</details>


### [117] [Noise-limited secret key agreement with twin optical physically unclonable functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04936)
*Georgios M. Nikolopoulos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于孪生光学PUF的硬件加密密钥生成与分发方案，提出了信息论安全的密钥协商协议，并探讨了其与QKD网络的集成可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用孪生光学PUF（相关物理不可克隆函数）作为硬件平台，实现无需计算假设或可信第三方的密码学密钥生成与分发，解决传统密钥管理中的安全性和可信性问题。

Method: 基于相关光学PUF产生的随机散斑图案，设计了包含安全草图、错误协调和隐私放大的信息论密钥协商协议；量化了公共辅助数据的信息泄露；推导了最终密钥长度的下界；分析了噪声区间内密钥协商的可行性，并评估了实用与近容量协调方案的性能。

Result: 确定了在何种噪声条件下可实现安全密钥协商；给出了最终密钥长度的下界；展示了实用与近容量协调方案的性能对比。

Conclusion: 孪生光学PUF可作为QKD网络中初始预共享密钥的建立机制，为两方提供不依赖计算假设或第三方的安全密钥协商方法。

Abstract: We investigate the use of twin optical fingerprints derived from correlated physical unclonable functions (PUFs), as a hardware-based platform for cryptographic key generation and distribution. Each fingerprint is associated with a random, yet reproducible speckle pattern, generated when coherent light is scattered by a disordered optical structure. We consider a pair of correlated optical PUFs, and study the conditions under which two honest parties can establish a common secret key, despite fabrication-induced variability and environmental noise. An explicit information-theoretic key-agreement protocol is developed, incorporating secure sketches, error reconciliation, and privacy amplification. We quantify information leakage due to public helper data, and derive lower bounds on the length of the final secret key. The analysis identifies the noise regimes in which secure key agreement is feasible, and examines the performance of both practical and near-capacity reconciliation schemes. Finally, we discuss how twin optical PUFs could be integrated into quantum key distribution (QKD) networks, as a mechanism for establishing an initial pre-shared secret key between two honest users, without relying on computational assumptions or trusted third parties.

</details>


### [118] [Contraction and Expansion Values of Quantum Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04950)
*Ruben Ibarrondo,Mikel Sanz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入"收缩值"和"扩张值"两个单调序列，以细化迹距离收缩系数，它们通过无迹厄米算子子空间上的极小-极大变分原理产生，具有操作解释，与Gel'fand数或Bernstein数一致，并建立了主要结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迹距离收缩系数作为极值比率，只能刻画量子信道最乐观的行为，即使对于非常嘈杂的信道也经常是平凡的，且单个标量不适合描述收缩在信道组合下的累积效应。

Method: 通过无迹厄米算子子空间上的极小-极大变分原理定义收缩值和扩张值两个单调序列，将其与Pietsch的s-数理论中的Gel'fand数或Bernstein数建立联系，并针对单量子比特信道、d维振幅阻尼信道和直和信道进行计算或估计。

Result: 收缩值和扩张值以奇异值细化算子范数的方式细化了收缩系数和扩张系数，在信道组合下提供了收缩系数单独无法提供的界限，具有明确的操作解释。

Conclusion: 所引入的序列在信道组合下提供了更精细的刻画，丰富了量子信息中信道收缩性质的分析工具。

Abstract: The contraction coefficient of the trace distance is a central tool in quantum information, quantifying how strongly a quantum channel degrades the distinguishability of states. However, being an extremal ratio, it captures only the most optimistic behaviour of the channel and is often trivial, even for very noisy channels. Moreover, a single scalar is poorly suited to describe how contraction accumulates under channel composition. In this work we introduce the \emph{contraction and expansion values}, two monotone sequences that refine the contraction and expansion coefficients in the same way singular values refine the operator norm. They arise from a min--max variational principle over subspaces of traceless Hermitian operators, admit an operational interpretation in terms of two state-discrimination games, and are shown to coincide with the Gel'fand or Bernstein numbers of the channel restricted to traceless operators. This identification places the sequences within Pietsch's theory of $s$-numbers and yields, in particular, bounds under channel composition that the contraction coefficient alone cannot provide. We establish their main structural properties and compute or estimate them for single-qubit channels, $d$-dimensional amplitude damping channels, and direct-sum channels.

</details>


### [119] [Extending the Bloch sphere model to an N-qubit system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04979)
*Francisco Piñero,Cristian Franco,Hernán I. de la Cruz,Fernando L. Pelayo,Vicente Pascual,Mauro Mezzini,Jose Javier Paulet,Fernando Cuartero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种将Bloch球面表示推广到任意N量子比特系统的几何模型，使用2^N-1个球面唯一描述多量子比特态，并通过受控旋转门层次化应用建立参数化方法，证明了与标准态矢量表示的严格双射关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bloch球面是表示单量子比特状态的优雅工具，但缺乏被广泛接受的多量子比特系统（含纠缠态）的推广表示方法。

Method: 提出使用2^N-1个球面的几何模型，将任意N量子比特纯态表示为多个球面的组合；对于2量子比特态，使用三个球面（两个单量子比特球面和一个纠缠球面）唯一描述；通过沿Z轴和Y轴的受控旋转门层次化应用实现N量子比特参数化。

Result: 严格证明了该模型的角度参数与标准态矢量表示之间存在双射关系，即模型能唯一且完整地表示任意N量子比特纯态。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了多量子比特纠缠的可视化直观理解，有望为量子模拟器带来计算优势，并为量子门分析提供新的视角。

Abstract: The Bloch sphere is an elegant tool for representing single-qubit states. However, a widely accepted generalization for multi-qubit systems with entanglement remains absent. We propose a novel geometric model extending the Bloch sphere representation to arbitrary $N$-qubit systems using $2^N-1$ spheres. We demonstrate that any pure 2-qubit state is uniquely described by three spheres: two for individual qubits and a third encapsulating bipartite entanglement. Generalizing this, we establish an $N$-qubit parameterization through the hierarchical application of controlled rotation gates along the $Z$ and $Y$ axes. We formally prove a strict bijection between the standard state vector representation and our model's angular parameters. This framework provides an intuitive visualization of multiple entanglement, offering potential computational advantages for quantum simulators and new analytical perspectives on quantum gates.

</details>


### [120] [Quantum Hashing via Constrained Rydberg Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04991)
*Han-Chao Chen,Xin Liu,Zheng-Yuan Zhang,Dong-Sheng Ding,Bao-Sen Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文表明里德伯原子阵列中的约束多体动力学能够自然产生量子哈希机制，通过将三元字符串编码为状态空间中的确定性轨迹，经典信息空间被映射到希尔伯特空间中的量子态系综，该系综具有高概率近似正交性、随机分布性和广泛几何覆盖性，这些几何特征自然带来了量子哈希的核心密码学特性（低碰撞概率、单向性、篡改敏感性和隐私保护），证明量子哈希的密码学功能无需依赖精心设计的算法，而是可以从约束多体动力学中自然涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子动力学本身是否能够作为一种物理资源，自然产生量子哈希的密码学功能，而不依赖于人为设计的算法。

Method: 利用里德伯原子阵列中的约束多体动力学，将三元字符串编码为状态空间中的确定性轨迹，将经典信息空间映射到希尔伯特空间中的量子态系综，并通过统计分析研究该系综的几何性质。

Result: 该量子态系综展现出高概率近似正交性、随机-like分布和广泛几何覆盖性，并由此自然具备低碰撞概率、单向性、篡改敏感性和隐私保护等密码学特性。

Conclusion: 量子哈希的密码学功能可以从约束多体动力学中自然涌现，无需依赖专门设计的算法，量子动力学本身即可作为密码学信息处理的物理资源。

Abstract: In this Letter, we show that constrained many-body dynamics in Rydberg atom arrays naturally gives rise to a quantum hashing mechanism. By encoding ternary strings into deterministic trajectories in the state space, the classical information space is mapped onto a quantum state ensemble in the Hilbert space with an induced geometric structure. Statistical analysis reveals that this ensemble exhibits high probability near-orthogonality, random-like distribution, and broad geometric coverage. These geometric features naturally give rise to the essential cryptographic properties of quantum hashing, including low collision probability, one-wayness, tamper sensitivity, and privacy preservation. Our results demonstrate that the cryptographic functionality of quantum hashing need not rely on deliberately engineered algorithms, but can instead emerge naturally from constrained many-body dynamics, identifying quantum dynamics itself as a physical resource for cryptographic information processing.

</details>


### [121] [Canonical quantization of neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05000)
*Alexander He,Nana Liu,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将经典机器学习的神经元通过正则量子化（canonical quantization）进行量子化，即把神经元的能量函数替换为量子哈密顿量，并通过矩阵泛函微积分应用激活函数，得到可在输入量子态上测量的激活可观测量。作者开发了混合量子-经典算法进行训练与评估，数值实验表明量子化神经元在表达能力上优于经典神经元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将正则量子化这一物理学中的系统化程序应用于机器学习的基本计算原语——神经元，从而为构建量子机器学习原语提供理论框架。核心动机是探索量子版本的神经元是否能够比经典神经元具有更强的表达能力，以适应量子数据的处理需求。

Method: 将神经元视为能量函数与激活函数的复合，通过正则量子化将能量函数替换为量子哈密顿量，并利用矩阵泛函微积分将激活函数作用于哈密顿量，得到激活可观测量。开发了混合量子-经典算法，包括测量激活可观测量和估计平方损失误差梯度的过程。梯度估计依赖经典随机采样、Hadamard测试和哈密顿模拟；激活可观测量测量依赖"one qumode"和Schroedingerization等量子算法。

Result: 数值实验表明，量子化神经元在代表性学习任务上相比对应的经典神经元展现出更强的表达能力。

Conclusion: 正则量子化为构建量子机器学习原语提供了一个有原则的框架，并为开发适用于量子数据的神经架构奠定了基础。

Abstract: Canonical quantization provides a systematic procedure for constructing quantum models from classical Hamiltonians. Here, we apply this principle to a fundamental computational primitive of machine learning: the neuron. Specifically, by viewing a neuron as a composition of an energy function and an activation function, we quantize this model by replacing the energy function with a quantum Hamiltonian and applying the activation function to it through matrix functional calculus. This results in an activation observable that can be measured on an input quantum state. We investigate the use of these quantized neurons for function approximation, where the objective is to learn an unknown observable from labeled quantum data. For this purpose, we develop hybrid quantum-classical algorithms for training and evaluation, including procedures for measuring the activation observable and estimating gradients of the squared loss error. Our algorithms for gradient estimation rely on basic primitives like classical random sampling, the Hadamard test, and Hamiltonian simulation, and those for measuring an activation observable rely on quantum algorithms known as the power of one qumode and Schroedingerization. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our quantized neurons exhibit enhanced expressive capabilities relative to corresponding classical neurons on representative learning tasks. Our work establishes canonical quantization as a principled framework for constructing quantum machine learning primitives and provides a foundation for developing neural architectures tailored to quantum data.

</details>


### [122] [Transmon Phase Gates Controlled by Superconducting Soliton DAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05072)
*Derek Reitz,Tony X. Zhou,Aditya Sharma,Ryan Bilotta,John McFarland,Aref Fouladi,Jacob Glasby,Aruna Ramanayaka,Zachary Stegen,Aaron Pesetski,Mark Covington,Gregory Boyd,Jeremy Clark*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种超导数模转换器（DAC），通过将梯形驱动脉冲转换为超导孤子并保持在负载回路中，实现对超导量子比特的纳秒级高保真门操作。实验展示了5.6 ns的S门，激发概率0.05%，但存在1.6%的非局域相位误差，需通过EMI抑制和DC可调耦合器解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超导量子比特控制方案存在控制噪声、缺乏本征复用、门操作速度慢、依赖CMOS控制兼容性差等问题，需要一种既能滤波噪声、提供本征复用、实现纳秒级门操作且与CMOS兼容的新型控制方案。

Method: 将梯形驱动脉冲转换为超导孤子，孤子保持在DAC负载回路中，通过互感对超导量子比特或门耦合器施加磁通。通过改变孤子保持时间或使用DC偏置可调DAC-量子比特耦合器来控制模拟磁通输出，从而实现固定时间、高保真度的量子门。

Result: 成功实现了5.6 ns的S门操作；DAC引起的量子态激发概率为0.05%；DAC引起的弛豫率低于transmon的固有T1极限；量子模拟表明通过过阻尼约瑟夫森结可将激发率再降低10倍；但扩展到多量子比特时，存在1.6%每门的非局域DAC诱导相位误差。

Conclusion: 该超导DAC方案能实现快速高保真量子门操作，但在多比特扩展中面临非局域电磁干扰导致的相位误差挑战。未来需通过优化芯片布局抑制EMI，并引入DC可调耦合器进行相位校正。

Abstract: We introduce a superconducting digital-to-analog converter (DAC) that filters control noise, provides native multiplexing, performs quantum gates in nanoseconds, and can be controlled by CMOS. This is achieved by transducing a trapezoidal drive pulse into a superconducting soliton, which is then held in the DAC load loop, applying flux to a mutually-coupled superconducting qubit or gate coupler. The analog flux output by the DAC can be easily controlled by varying the soliton hold time, or with a DC-biased tunable DAC-qubit coupler, allowing the DAC to perform a fixed-time, high-fidelity gate that's robust to fabrication variance or flux offsets in the quantum circuit. Our initial demonstration shows that the DAC can successfully perform 5.6 ns S-gates on transmons. We measure the DAC-induced quantum state excitation probability per gate to be 0.05%, and find that the DAC-induced relaxation rate from the qubit 1 state is below the intrinsic T1 rate limit of the transmon. Quantum simulations show qualitative agreement with the measured data, and predict that the DAC excitation rate can be lowered 10 times further by overdamping the Josephson junction (JJ) in the DAC load loop. may be limited by a Interleaved Randomized Benchmarking (IRB) sequences on an observer qubit reveal that, when scaling to many qubits, the DAC's performance may be limited by a non-local, DAC-induced phase error of 1.6% per gate, appearing in ancilla qubits that are not directly coupled to any of the 30 DACs on the chip. We discuss strategies for future layouts of multi-DAC chips that focus on mitigating the source of these non-local, high-frequency electromagnetic interactions (EMI), and how to incorporate a DC-tunable coupler for phase correction.

</details>


### [123] [Brownian Motion in Orthogonal and Symplectic Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05094)
*Zhiyang Tan,Piet W. Brouwer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了正交系综和辛系综布朗运动（描述粒子-空穴对称系统）的统一图解框架，计算了至四阶的多项式平均，构建了正交群中不连通SO^-(q)部分的正交不变插值，并探讨了在量子信息、量子混沌和量子输运中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵布朗运动是研究量子混沌、量子输运中的交叉系综以及多体动力学中热化和信息 scrambling 的强大框架，但此前正交系综和辛系综（描述粒子-空穴对称系统）的统一处理方法尚不完善。

Method: 发展统一图解框架，计算至四阶的多项式平均，构建正交群不连通SO^-(q)部分的正交不变插值。

Result: 完成了正交和辛随机矩阵布朗运动的统一图解表征，获得了至四阶的多项式平均值，并实现了正交群SO^-(q)部分的正交不变插值。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子信息、量子混沌和量子输运等领域提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Matrix Brownian motion provides a powerful framework for studying crossover ensembles in quantum chaos and quantum transport, as well as thermalization and information scrambling in many-body dynamics. Here, we develop a unified diagrammatic framework to characterize Brownian ensembles for orthogonal and symplectic random matrices, which describe systems with particle-hole symmetry. We compute polynomial averages up to fourth order and construct an orthogonally invariant interpolation for the disconnected $\mathrm{SO}^-(q)$ sector of the orthogonal group. We consider applications relating to the fields of quantum information, quantum chaos, and quantum transport.

</details>


### [124] [Complementary 3D color codes for transversal quantum logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05107)
*Friederike Butt,Luis Colmenarez,Erik Weilandt,Tom Peham,Robert Wille,Markus Müller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于四面体三维色码及其Hadamard变换码（H-四面体码）的混合架构，利用互补的横向非Clifford操作和位Hadamard变换实现几乎通用的横向逻辑门集，并通过容错的round-robin构造完成通用门集，其中魔法态和大部分纠缠操作是横向的，资源开销集中在少数非横向Clifford纠缠操作上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eastin-Knill定理禁止单一量子纠错码拥有通用横向逻辑门集，因此需要设计混合架构来绕过这一限制，实现容错量子计算。

Method: 1) 提出四面体三维色码和H-四面体码两种编码方案，支持互补的横向非Clifford操作；2) 利用位Hadamard变换在两种编码间切换；3) 实现从四面体码到H-四面体码的单向横向逻辑CNOT门；4) 通过分段容错的round-robin构造实现两个H-四面体码之间的逻辑受控-Z门；5) 采用二维色码逻辑辅助量子比特进行Steane型症候提取以降低开销。

Result: 实现了几乎通用的横向逻辑门集，支持纠缠态和魔法态的创建；通过非横向Clifford纠缠操作完善了通用门集，将资源开销集中在少数操作上。

Conclusion: 该架构为实现经典难模拟量子算法提供了新路径，其中魔法态和大部分纠缠操作保持横向特性，显著降低了容错开销。

Abstract: Transversal logical gates provide a direct route to fault-tolerant quantum computation, but the Eastin-Knill theorem forbids a universal transversal gate set within a single quantum error-correcting code. We propose a hybrid architecture based on the tetrahedral three-dimensional color code and its Hadamard-transformed counterpart, which we call the H-tetrahedral code. The two encodings support complementary transversal non-Clifford operations. Combined with bitwise Hadamard transformations that switch between the two encodings and a one-way transversal logical CNOT from the tetrahedral code to the H-tetrahedral code, these operations realize an almost-universal transversal logical gate set that enables both the creation of entanglement and logical states with magic. We complete a universal gate set through a pieceably fault-tolerant round-robin construction of a logical controlled-$Z$ gate between two H-tetrahedral codes. This logical entangling gate is interleaved with reduced-overhead Steane-type syndrome extraction using logical two-dimensional color-code auxiliary qubits. Our construction provides a new route toward implementing classically hard-to-simulate quantum algorithms where magic and most entangling operations are transversal while the resource overhead is concentrated in a small number of non-transversal Clifford entangling operations.

</details>


### [125] [Characterisation of a satellite-to-ground channel for continuous variable quantum key distribution protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05109)
*Emma Tien Hwai Medlock,Vinod N. Roa,Timothy Spiller,Rupesh Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于空间平台的连续变量量子密钥分发（CV-QKD）中动态量子信道的损耗特性，分析了随天顶角变化的信道损耗，并评估了在不同湍流强度和波长下生成正安全密钥的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在空间基CV-QKD协议中，量子信道具有动态特性，信道损耗随天顶角变化，这会导致参数估计问题，特别是对于传输本地振荡器方案，还会引起散粒噪声波动。因此，表征信道损耗及其来源至关重要。

Method: 在实用假设下对变化的信道损耗进行表征，考虑了不同场景、湍流强度以及波长条件。

Result: 对于所考虑的信道参数，在限制性Eve安全假设下，可以生成正安全密钥。

Conclusion: 动态信道损耗特性已被表征，且在特定安全假设下可实现安全密钥生成。

Abstract: In space based quantum key distribution (QKD) protocols, the quantum channel will be dynamic in nature and the channel loss will change with respect to the zenith angle. In the context of continuous variable (CV)-QKD, this will cause issues with parameter estimation and for a transmitted local oscillator in particular it will also fluctuate the shot noise. Therefore, it is vital to characterise this channel loss and the sources of this loss. In this paper the varying channel loss is characterised under practical assumptions. This is shown for various different scenarios, turbulence strengths, as well as wavelengths. This work shows, for the channel parameters considered, it is possible to generate a positive secret key if restricted Eve security assumptions are made.

</details>


### [126] [Fragile single-cone Dirac quantum walks in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05112)
*C. W. J. Beenakker,J. Sánchez Férnan,J. Tworzydło*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现二维量子行走中，类似一维情况下无费米子加倍的无质量狄拉克方程离散化构建更加脆弱：局部两带量子行走虽能产生未配对的狄拉克锥，但保护对称性变为非简单空间群对称性（涉及半格点平移），会被空间非均匀性破坏。基于Ho-Chalker网络模型的二维狄拉克量子行走可被势散射打开能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究二维量子行走是否能像一维情况那样，在保持基本对称性（手征性和时间反演）的前提下，实现无费米子加倍的低能狄拉克费米子离散化。

Method: 理论分析二维局部两带量子行走的对称性结构，特别是非简单空间群对称性（半格点平移）对狄拉克锥的保护作用，并以Ho-Chalker网络模型为例进行具体分析。

Result: 二维局部两带量子行走可以存在未配对的狄拉克锥，但保护对称性不再是普通的在位对称性，而是涉及半格点平移的非简单空间群对称性；这些对称性会被空间非均匀性（如势散射）破坏，导致狄拉克锥打开能隙。

Conclusion: 二维狄拉克量子行走的构建比一维情况更脆弱，Ho-Chalker网络模型中的狄拉克锥可以通过势散射被打开能隙，表明二维量子行走难以像一维那样稳定实现无费米子加倍的低能狄拉克费米子。

Abstract: It is known that a one-dimensional (1D) quantum walk gives a local space-time discretization of the massless Dirac equation with a single quasi-energy cone (no fermion doubling at low energies), keeping the fundamental symmetries (chiral and time-reversal) of the continuum theory. We show that the analogous 2D construction is fundamentally more fragile. Local two-band quantum walks can have an unpaired Dirac cone, but the protecting symmetries then cease to be ordinary on-site symmetries: they become non-symmorphic, involving half-lattice translations, and are broken by generic spatial inhomogeneities. In particular, we demonstrate that the 2D Dirac quantum walk based on the Ho-Chalker network model can be gapped by potential scattering.

</details>


### [127] [Efficient classical simulation of two-dimensional long-range systems: Rydberg arrays and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05178)
*Jia-Lin Chan,Tao Xiang,Yantao Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的张量网络变分蒙特卡洛框架，将具有任意全连二体相互作用的N-体系量子系统的局域能量计算从O(N³)降至O(N)，成功模拟了此前无法经典模拟的10×10偶极XY模型的绝热实时动力学，并解决了从乘积态演化的病理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分蒙特卡洛(VMC)计算中，评估具有任意全连二体相互作用的N-体系量子系统的局域能量通常需要O(N³)的计算成本，限制了可模拟的系统规模。

Method: 提出了一种新的张量网络VMC框架，将局域能量计算复杂度从O(N³)降低到O(N)，并解决了从乘积态演化的病理问题。

Result: 成功模拟了10×10偶极XY模型的绝热实时协议，该结果此前无法通过经典模拟实现；同时进行了基态VMC计算与绝热态制备进行比较。

Conclusion: 张量网络VMC是长程量子平台（如里德伯和离子阱模拟器）的强大经典模拟工具，为当前亟需可扩展经典基准测试工具的实验提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In variational Monte Carlo (VMC) calculations of $N$-site quantum systems with arbitrary all-to-all two-body interactions, evaluating the local energy generally costs $O(N^3)$. We introduce a new framework that reduces this cost to $O(N)$ for tensor network states, capable of scalable and accurate computation of real-time dynamics and ground states. As a result, we obtain accurate simulations of the adiabatic real-time protocol of a $10\times10$ dipolar XY model realized in a Rydberg simulator [C. Chen et al., Nature 616, 691 (2023)], which was previously beyond the reach of classical simulation. Going beyond quantum experiments, we also directly perform ground state VMC to compare with the adiabatic state preparation. Our work demonstrates tensor network VMC as a powerful classical simulator for long-range quantum platforms such as Rydberg and ion-trap simulators, which are currently in urgent need of scalable classical benchmarking tools. As a separate technical contribution, we resolve the pathology of evolving from product states within of tensor network VMC.

</details>


### [128] [Quasi-holonomy in non-adiabatic quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05218)
*Erik Sjöqvist,Adam Fredriksson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在复Grassmann流形上子空间的非绝热量子时间演化中准和乐（quasi-holonomy）的理论框架。通过将薛定谔演化分解为动力学部分和移动基下由联络诱导的部分，得到了一个显式依赖于动力学传播子的有效几何生成元。该准联络不构成原始Grassmann丛上的真正联络，因为其规范变换律包含历史依赖的非局域项。文章还简要讨论了其他分解薛定谔演化的方式，这些方法都存在类似的历史依赖性，因此定义的子空间输运中几何效应与动力学效应通常是纠缠的。工作从规范理论视角揭示了将子空间量子演化分离为和乐部分与动力学部分的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非绝热条件下量子子空间演化中几何（和乐）与动力学效应的分离问题，现有绝热和乐框架在非绝热情况下不再适用，需要发展新的理论框架来理解一般情况下的几何相位与子空间输运。

Method: 在移动基下将薛定谔演化分解为动力学传播子贡献和联络诱导贡献，从而定义出准联络与准和乐；通过规范变换分析揭示该构造的历史依赖性；并与其他分解方案进行比较。

Result: 得到的准联络不满足真正联络的规范变换律，其变换包含历史依赖的非局域项；所有分解方案都无法完全分离几何和动力学效应，两者在非绝热情况下本质上纠缠在一起。

Conclusion: 从规范理论视角揭示了非绝热子空间演化中几何与动力学效应的一般性纠缠特性，指出了在非绝热情况下无法严格区分和乐与动力学部分的根本困难。

Abstract: We develop a framework for quasi-holonomy in non-adiabatic quantum time evolution of subspaces along loops in a complex Grassmannian. By factoring the Schrödinger evolution into dynamical and connection-induced contributions in a moving basis, we obtain an effective geometric generator that depends explicitly on the dynamical propagator. This quasi-connection does not define a genuine connection on the original Grassmann bundle, since its gauge transformation law acquires a history-dependent, nonlocal term. Other ways of factoring the Schrödinger evolution are briefly discussed. All these approaches suffer from the same type of history-dependence, thereby defining transport of subspaces in which geometric and dynamical effects are generally intertwined, just as in the case of the quasi-holonomy. Our work sheds light on the issue of separating quantum evolution of subspaces into holonomic and dynamical parts from an essentially gauge-theoretic perspective.

</details>


### [129] [Routing Anonymity and Identifiability of Noisy Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05281)
*Ben Priestley,Mina Doosti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了量子云计算后端可识别性（backend identifiability）的形式化框架，提出了路由匿名性（routing anonymity）作为量子云服务的安全概念。通过假设检验理论，证明在被动独立同分布访问下路由匿名性以Chernoff速率指数衰减，并建立了效用-匿名性权衡。实验在Amazon Braket上实现，对超导量子处理器分类准确率87-90%，跨物理平台分类达96-100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算基于云端，用户提交电路到服务商的专有后端硬件。服务商可能希望隐藏实现细节、调度选择甚至物理设备，但含噪声的有限次输出会携带后端特有的指纹信息，可能泄露后端身份。此前研究多从基准测试角度关注指纹，忽视了用户和提供商的隐私问题。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 定义了后端可识别性游戏来形式化路由匿名性；将后端可识别性建模为假设检验问题，利用Chernoff信息论工具证明匿名性指数衰减；建立效用-匿名性权衡；利用Pauli传递矩阵工具提出深度原理，说明指纹是中间深度现象；在Amazon Braket上使用离子阱和超导量子处理器进行实验验证。

Result: 实验显示：超导量子后端之间分类准确率87-90%，不同物理平台（离子阱 vs 超导）分类准确率96-100%，且可识别性在自然后处理下仍能保持。

Conclusion: 路由匿名性是量子云计算中一个独立的安全需求，本文提供了量化与控制效用-匿名性权衡的理论框架。

Abstract: Present-day quantum computing is cloud-based, where a user submits a circuit to a service provider's proprietary backend hardware. While providers may wish to hide implementation details, scheduling choices, or even which physical device was used, noisy finite-shot outputs can carry backend-specific fingerprints: information imprinted in the classical output distribution that can reveal the backend identity. So far, such fingerprints have mostly been studied from a benchmarking perspective, with limited attention to privacy considerations for users and providers. This work develops the first formal framework for backend identifiability and its privacy implications. We introduce a backend-identifiability game and use it to formalise routing anonymity as a security notion for quantum cloud services. We show that backend identifiability is a hypothesis-testing problem and prove that, under passive i.i.d. access to a single backend, routing anonymity decays exponentially at the Chernoff rate. We also establish a utility-anonymity trade-off, imposing fundamental limits on how much backend-specific information can be removed from classical outputs without degrading their usefulness. In addition, we observe that, for noisy quantum hardware, identifying fingerprints are inherently an intermediate-depth phenomenon, and establish a depth principle using Pauli-transfer-matrix tools. We complement the theory with experiments on Amazon Braket on AWS, using ion-trap and superconducting quantum processors. We observe 87-90% classification between superconducting backends and 96-100% classification across physical platforms, and find that identifiability can survive natural forms of post-processing. Overall, these results establish routing anonymity as a distinct security requirement for quantum cloud computing, and provide a framework for quantifying and controlling the utility-anonymity trade-off.

</details>


### [130] [Quantum Spectral Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05307)
*Yewei Yuan,Michele Minervini,Mark M. Wilde,Nana Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子谱异常检测（QSPADE），通过平均态的谱直接计算类似PCA的异常分数，用平滑温度控制谱阈值替代硬PCA秩选择，实现了零温极限下恢复硬投影PCA分数，且样本复杂度与数据维度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子异常检测中，经典PCA方法因量子数据缺乏标准中心化、显式恢复主特征向量或构建完整Gram矩阵成本过高而效率低下，亟需一种不依赖上述昂贵步骤的量子异常检测方法。

Method: QSPADE通过计算正常数据集平均态的谱，直接得到PCA类异常分数。用温度控制的平滑谱阈值替代硬PCA秩选择，使近阈值谱分量部分贡献于异常分数，从而避免硬截止带来的跳跃和噪声敏感问题。

Result: 零温极限下QSPADE恢复硬投影PCA分数；基于测量的量子检测器样本复杂度与数据维度无关；数值模拟显示其在编码经典数据上行为类似核PCA，且能在无预定义序参量的情况下检测横向场伊辛相变。

Conclusion: QSPADE为编码经典数据的量子核异常检测和量子原生系统的监测提供了高效框架，尤其适用于诊断观测量未知的场景。

Abstract: A core task in quantum anomaly detection is to compute an anomaly score that quantifies how strongly a test quantum state deviates from a given quantum dataset assumed to be normal. Classically, principal component analysis (PCA) for centered data computes the anomaly score by evaluating the test sample relative to the subspace spanned by the selected leading eigenvectors. However, for quantum data that lack a standard centering, explicitly recovering principal eigenvectors, constructing full Gram matrices, or loading quantum-random-access-memory-style data can be more costly than estimating the anomaly score itself. To avoid these costs, we propose Quantum Spectral Anomaly Detection (QSPADE), which computes PCA-like anomaly scores directly from the spectrum of the average state of the normal dataset. By replacing hard PCA rank selection with a smooth, temperature-controlled spectral threshold, QSPADE makes near-threshold spectral components contribute partially to the anomaly score. This makes the score vary continuously rather than jump when a borderline component is included or excluded, and makes it less sensitive to noise or arbitrary hard cutoffs near the threshold. In the zero-temperature limit, QSPADE recovers the hard-projector PCA score. The proposed measurement-based quantum detector can be calibrated with a sample complexity independent of the data dimension. Numerical simulations show that QSPADE behaves like kernel-PCA on encoded classical data and detects changes across a transverse-field Ising transition without predefined order parameters. Consequently, QSPADE gives an efficient framework for both quantum-kernel anomaly detection on encoded classical data and the monitoring of quantum-native systems where diagnostic observables are unknown.

</details>


### [131] [Quantum Computational Resources and Conformal Field Theory: Unifying Spins, Bosons, and Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05343)
*Ryota Matsuda,Masahiro Hoshino,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了“魔法Rényi熵”（MRE）作为统一度量，用于在自旋、玻色子和费米子系统中量化计算资源（非稳定子性和非高斯性）。通过共形场论（CFT）分析，发现MRE的普适贡献由Affleck-Ludwig边界熵决定，非高斯性可连续重整化该贡献或驱动边界相变。以Tomonaga-Luttinger液体为例，展示了边界相变点，并用数值计算验证了场论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明量子纠缠和量子魔法（magic）能从互补角度刻画多体量子态；然而在玻色子和费米子系统中，尚缺乏类似自旋系统中用于量化非高斯性（计算资源）的统一场论框架。本文致力于填补这一空白。

Method: 引入魔法Rényi熵（MRE）作为统一度量，通过共形场论（CFT）分析得出MRE的普适贡献由Affleck-Ludwig边界熵确定。以Tomonaga-Luttinger液体中相互作用的无自旋费米子为例，进行详细的CFT分析和数值计算验证。

Result: 发现非高斯性可连续重整化MRE的普适贡献或通过体诱导的边界重整化群流驱动边界相变；在Luttinger参数K=1/3和K=3处观察到边界相变。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了跨越自旋、玻色子和费米子系统的多体魔法的统一场论理解，揭示了非稳定子性和非高斯性在临界多体态中的共同普适特征。

Abstract: Characterizing a quantum state through the lens of quantum resources provides an information-theoretic perspective on many-body systems. While quantum entanglement serves as the paradigmatic example of a quantum resource, recent studies have shown that quantum magic, a resource for universal quantum computation, can capture aspects of many-body states complementary to those described by entanglement. For instance, in spin systems, conformal field theory (CFT) analysis of the stabilizer Rényi entropy has revealed universal features of nonstabilizerness that are qualitatively distinct from entanglement. In bosonic and fermionic systems, however, a comparable formulation for their computational resource, non-Gaussianity, has yet to be established. In this work, we introduce a unified measure, the magic Rényi entropy (MRE), to quantify computational resources in spins, bosons, and fermions on an equal footing. This allows us to reveal common universal aspects of nonstabilizerness and non-Gaussianity in critical many-body states. In particular, our CFT analysis shows that the universal contribution to the MRE appears as the size-independent term determined by the Affleck-Ludwig boundary entropy. We find that non-Gaussianity can continuously renormalize this universal contribution or drive a boundary phase transition through bulk-induced boundary renormalization-group flows. As a concrete demonstration, we present a detailed CFT analysis of non-Gaussianity in interacting spinless fermions described by the Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid, showing boundary transitions at the Luttinger parameters $K=1/3$ and $K=3$. We perform numerical calculations that confirm our field-theoretical predictions. These results provide a unified field-theoretical understanding of many-body magic across spins, bosons, and fermions.

</details>


### [132] [Coherent Control of Energy Transport at Room Temperature in a Noisy Bath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05361)
*Davinder Singh,Paul Brumer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在非平衡稳态（NESS）中，通过相干控制反应中心连接的供体-受体对来实现能量传输的调控。供体-受体对持续与非相干辐射和声子浴相互作用，同时受相位控制的相干场驱动。相干激发诱导激发路径间的干涉，实现通量的相位依赖调制，从而增强或抑制能量转移，实现光学能量开关。这种干涉在耗散体系NESS中的持续存在，表明量子控制的操作范围可从传统瞬态低耗散域扩展至室温噪声环境下的NESS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子控制主要应用于低耗散的瞬态领域，而现实光合作用等体系在室温噪声环境下往往处于非平衡稳态。本文旨在探索能否将相干控制拓展到这类强耗散、连续驱动的NESS体系中，实现室温下的能量流调控。

Method: 构建反应中心连接的供体-受体对模型，让色素分子持续与非相干辐射（模拟太阳光）和声子浴相互作用，同时施加相位可控的相干激光场。通过分析激发路径间的量子干涉效应，研究相干场相位对能量通量的调制作用。

Result: 相干激发在供体-受体对中诱导了激发路径间的干涉，使得能量通量出现相位依赖的调制。通过调节相干场相位，可以选择性增强或抑制能量转移，实现类似光学开关的功能。这种干涉效应在耗散环境下的非平衡稳态中依然能够保持。

Conclusion: 相干控制在耗散性非平衡稳态中仍然可行，为量子控制从传统低耗散瞬态领域向室温噪声环境下的稳态调控扩展提供了理论依据，对人工光合作用、量子热力学和量子器件设计具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Coherent control of energy transport in a non-equilibrium steady-state (NESS) in a reaction-center-connected donor-acceptor pair is proposed. The pigments are considered to be continuously interacting with incoherent radiation and a phonon bath while being driven by phase-controlled coherent fields. Coherent excitation of the donor-acceptor pair is shown to induce interference between excitation pathways, resulting in phase dependent modulation of the flux. As a consequence one can enhance or suppress energy transfer via interference, e.g. an optical energy switch. The persistence of such interference enables coherent control at a NESS in dissipative regime suggests an extension of the operational scope of quantum control from traditional transient domain with low dissiaption to noisy environment NESS at room temperature.

</details>


### [133] [Logical Spectroscopy: Lifted-Product Codes with Addressable Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05386)
*Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"逻辑光谱学"方法，利用中国剩余定理将阿贝尔提升积码的群代数分解为Frobenius特征包，在每个包内进行有限域计算（核、商、同调），再通过CRT幂等元提升回原空间，从而为有限阿贝尔提升积码构造出完整的、可寻址的共轭逻辑基，并给出设计诊断指标（包秩、基宽度上界、擦除属性分析）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图积（HGP）码的逻辑结构透明，因为其种子矩阵是二进制的，可在域上做行化简；但阿贝尔提升积码的种子条目是群代数环中的移位（或移位稀疏和），枢轴块未必可逆，全局行化简失效，导致难以显式构造共轭逻辑算符。因此需要一种替代方法来实现逻辑基的显式构造与寻址。

Method: 利用中国剩余定理（CRT）将阿贝尔提升群的群代数分解为Frobenius特征包（有限域上的直和），在每个特征包内独立进行有限域上的核、商和乘积复形同调计算，再将计算结果通过CRT幂等元提升回原群代数空间，通过互逆迹对偶包配对X和Z逻辑算符，最终得到完整的可寻址共轭逻辑基。

Result: 为有限阿贝尔提升积码LP(A,B)构造了完整的可寻址共轭逻辑基；包秩揭示了逻辑扇区的分裂方式；提升表示给出了构造的共轭基宽度的认证上界；整轨道擦除可分解为包属性擦除逻辑维度。在种子形状和有界群基支撑假设下，该构造赋予了阿贝尔提升积qLDPC码族类似于HGP码的特征。

Conclusion: CRT特征包不仅作为计算工具，还充当工作坐标系：它们标记逻辑扇区、认证构造的基宽度、归因结构化擦除失效。该方法在保留群代数提升布局自由度的同时，赋予了阿贝尔提升积码HGP式的逻辑结构透明度。

Abstract: Quantum LDPC memories can encode many logical qubits, but dimension alone does not make them usable: applications need explicit conjugate logical operators with structured labels and physical representatives. For hypergraph-product (HGP) codes this structure is transparent, since the input matrices are binary and can be row-reduced over $\mathbb{F}_2$. Abelian lifted-product codes are subtler. Their seed entries are shifts, or sparse sums of shifts, in a group-algebra ring rather than a field, so pivot blocks need not be invertible and global row reduction can fail.
  We address this with \emph{logical spectroscopy}, a spectral construction that replaces global row reduction by finite-field computations in the Frobenius character packets of the Abelian lift group. The Chinese remainder theorem (CRT) decomposes the group algebra into these packets. In each packet, we compute kernels, quotients, and product-complex homology; we then lift the resulting representatives back with CRT idempotents and pair $X$ and $Z$ logicals through reciprocal trace-dual packets. This gives complete addressable conjugate logical bases for finite Abelian lifted products $\mathsf{LP}(A,B)$.
  The same packet data also gives design diagnostics. Packet ranks show how logical sectors split, the lifted representatives give certified upper bounds on the width of the constructed conjugate basis, and whole-orbit erasures decompose into packet-attributed erased-logical dimensions. Thus, CRT packets also serve as working coordinates: they label logical sectors, certify the constructed basis width, and attribute structured erasure failures. Under bounded seed-shape and group-basis-support assumptions, this construction gives Abelian lifted-product qLDPC families an HGP-like feature while preserving the layout freedom of group-algebra lifts.

</details>
